Theorem 1

Statement: In Cognitive, consider the structural property of object 1.

Proof: By mathematical induction on 1. Base case: for 1 = 1, the result follows from the definition. Inductive step: assuming the property holds for all values up to 1, we observe that the algebraic structure preserves the invariant. Applying the closure axiom and the homomorphism property, we obtain the desired equality. This completes the proof. ∎
